## Devilish Beauty Lipstick Advertisement Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-end, hyper-realistic beauty advertisement shot.

Composition:
Side-view composition of an attractive brunette woman, positioned slightly in profile. She is leaning her back confidently against a gigantic red lipstick, scaled dramatically larger than life. Her arms are crossed, posture strong and elegant, while she turns her head to look directly at the camera with a bold, self-assured expression.

Appearance:
She has smooth, luminous skin, deep brown hair styled sleek and polished, and striking red lipstick that perfectly matches the oversized product behind her. Her facial features are symmetrical and refined, with confident eyes and a subtle, knowing gaze.
She wears a minimalist black dress, form-fitting, elegant, and timeless.

Product:
The oversized lipstick is bright red, glossy, perfectly cylindrical, clearly branded {argument name="brand" default="MAC"} with realistic logo placement and premium material finish.

Text Element:
At the top of the image, the slogan “{argument name="slogan" default="Devilish Beauty"}” is written as if handwritten with red lipstick—bold, sensual strokes, slightly imperfect, glossy texture, natural lipstick smudge edges.

Background:
Pure white studio background, seamless, high-key, no visible shadows or distractions.

Lighting:
Professional studio lighting—soft key light on her face, gentle rim light to define her silhouette, subtle reflections on the lipstick tube. Clean, luxury cosmetic ad lighting.

Style & Quality:
Ultra-photorealistic, luxury fashion photography, crisp focus, natural skin texture, premium cosmetics campaign aesthetic, editorial magazine quality.

Negative Prompt; anime, illustration, CGI, cartoon, flat lighting, low detail, blurry, distorted anatomy, plastic skin, over-smoothed face, low-budget ad, cluttered background
```

## 8-Frame Fitness Storytelling Collage

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"A cinematic 8-photo storytelling collage featuring the same girl throughout the journey.

Frame 1: Early morning at home — the girl steps out of her house holding her bicycle, calm and motivated.

Frame 2: She cycles through quiet streets, soft morning light, motion blur on wheels.

Frame 3: Arrival at the gym — she parks the cycle outside the gym entrance, focused expression.

Frame 4: Inside the gym — light warm-up stretches, gym equipment visible in background.

Frame 5: Intense workout moment — lifting weights or using machines, sweat and effort visible.

Frame 6: Post-workout relief — she drinks a protein shake, relaxed and satisfied.

Frame 7: Leaving the gym — gym bag on shoulder, confident walk out.

Frame 8: Cycling back home during golden hour, peaceful and accomplished mood.

Consistent outfit progression (casual cycling wear to gym wear), realistic body movement, cinematic lighting, shallow depth of field, natural colors, lifestyle fitness photography style, editorial storytelling layout, ultra-realistic detail, 1:1 aspect ratio." 

A cinematic 8-photo storytelling collage featuring the **same girl** throughout her full fitness journey – perfect character consistency across all frames!  
  
**Frame 1:** Early morning at home — she steps out holding her bicycle, calm and motivated.  
**Frame 2:** Cycles through quiet streets, soft morning light, motion blur on wheels.  
**Frame 3:** Arrival at gym — parks the bike outside entrance, focused expression.  
**Frame 4:** Inside gym — light warm-up stretches, equipment in background.  
**Frame 5:** Intense workout — lifting weights/machines, sweat and effort visible.  
**Frame 6:** Post-workout — drinks protein shake, relaxed and satisfied.  
**Frame 7:** Leaving gym — bag on shoulder, confident walk.  
**Frame 8:** Cycling home in golden hour, peaceful and accomplished.

**Style:** Consistent outfit progression (cycling wear to gym), realistic movement, cinematic lighting, shallow DoF, natural colors, lifestyle fitness photography, editorial layout, ultra-realistic detail, 1:1 ratio.​​​​
```

## Cinematic Rainy Night Street Portrait of a Confident Man

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Concept & Mood

Dramatic cinematic street portrait with quiet confidence and controlled intensity. Moody, rain-soaked atmosphere balanced by inner strength and self-assurance. Feels like a decisive moment — not a goodbye of loss, but of purpose. Urban power, restraint, and masculine composure.

Subject & Anatomy

Adult man with realistic anatomy and natural proportions.
Lean-to-average build, upright posture with relaxed but grounded shoulders.
Stance conveys stability and certainty rather than hesitation.
No exaggerated posing — strength expressed through stillness.

Pose & Perspective

The subject stands alone on a rain-soaked city street at night.
One hand holds a {argument name="umbrella color" default="black"} umbrella, angled slightly back to clearly reveal the face.
Body facing forward, head subtly turned toward the camera.
Camera positioned close at eye level for authority and presence.
Tight medium close-up to close-up framing with facial dominance.

Face & Expression (Primary Focus)

Face is the dominant visual anchor.
Expression is calm, confident, and emotionally controlled.
Eyes steady and focused, looking forward or slightly past the camera.
Brows neutral with subtle firmness, no tension.
Lips relaxed with a hint of determination — no smile, no sadness.
Rain beads on skin and eyelashes, enhancing cinematic realism without softening strength.

Skin (Key Focus)

Highly realistic skin texture with:

visible pores

natural imperfections

subtle tonal variation

realistic rain interaction

Skin appears natural and slightly wet, not glossy.
No smoothing, no artificial glow, no beauty retouching.


Hair & Grooming

Dark hair slightly damp from rain, natural clumping and weight.
Strands rest realistically on the forehead and temples.
Well-groomed short beard with clean but soft edges.
No stylized grooming — practical, masculine realism.

Wardrobe & Textiles

Dark navy wool overcoat as the hero garment.
Heavy fabric with visible wool texture and structure.
Rain-darkened areas and natural water absorption visible.
Black trousers beneath, understated and clean.
Leather pumpi boots with subtle wet sheen and grounded stance.
No logos, no branding.

Accessories

Matte black umbrella with visible rain runoff along the edges.
Umbrella slightly translucent under streetlight glow.
Accessories remain minimal and functional.

Environment

Rainy urban street at night.
Wet asphalt with strong cinematic reflections of streetlights.
Soft bokeh of distant city lights and blurred architecture.
Environment enhances authority and presence, not loneliness.

Lighting

Low-key cinematic lighting.
Soft directional key light defining facial structure.
Cool ambient tones balanced with restrained warm highlights.
Rain illuminated as fine streaks and droplets.
No flash, no studio look.

Camera & Optics

Photographer mindset — cinematic authority.
Full-frame look, 50mm–85mm portrait lens.
Shallow d
```

## Film Noir Cinematic Portrait Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-contrast cinematic black-and-white studio portrait of a man in the uploaded image with an athletic build. Three-quarter body portrait, vertical 3:4 aspect ratio. He stands left-of-center, torso slightly angled, head turned to the left, gazing off-camera with a serious, contemplative expression. Full, well-groomed beard, textured hair, intense focused eyes with glasses. He’s wearing a slim-fit dark long-sleeve henley shirt in a charcoal-to-black tone. Accessories include a wristwatch on his left wrist, a bracelet on his right wrist, and a ring on his right hand. Shot from a slightly low angle. Dramatic low-key cinematic lighting with a strong rear-right side light creating bold rim highlights along the jawline, shoulders, and arms. Minimal to no fill light, deep shadows, high contrast. A visible studio light appears in the background, with light haze or smoke adding atmospheric diffusion. Studio environment with a soft, smoky, obscured background and significant dark negative space on the right side of the frame. Subject in sharp focus with shallow to moderate depth of field, background softly diffused. Subtle film grain, wide dynamic range, rich blacks, detailed mid-gray skin tones, bright edge highlights. Moody, intense, film-noir-inspired editorial portrait suggesting strength, introspection, and quiet tension.
```

## Avant-Garde Ghost Duplicate Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"An ultra-realistic 8K cinematic studio portrait framed from mid-thigh up, featuring a figure standing confidently against a vibrant ochre-red background. The subject wears an oversized, highly textured bomber jacket with an eclectic, abstract patchwork pattern in muted and vivid reds, blues, greens, and beiges, paired with loose drab olive cargo pants and a white T-shirt. Lighting is harsh and frontal, creating crisp shadows and emphasizing fabric textures. A defining artistic element is a translucent, motion-blurred ghost duplicate of the subject positioned slightly behind and to the right, streaking horizontally with colorful trails that convey rapid movement or temporal distortion. The background remains uniform but subtly graded, adding depth without distraction. Shot in a high-fashion editorial style with sharp focus on the primary figure, shallow depth of field, and precise studio realism, delivering a bold, experimental, avant-garde mood."
```

## Ultra-Photorealistic Team USA Track Athletes Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An ultra-photorealistic, high-end editorial sports portrait shot on a Medium Format Phase One XF IQ4 camera with an 80mm lens, capturing a razor-sharp full-body image of two elite track athletes in a professional studio. The aesthetic is clean, cinematic, and incredibly detailed, strictly avoiding any plastic, waxy, or artificial smoothing. The background is a seamless minimalist cyclorama with a smooth monochromatic cyan-light blue gradient. Professional softbox lighting creates flattering highlights on muscle definition and skin, casting soft, natural shadows on the floor.
The subject on the left is a tall, highly muscular male athlete with a deep skin tone and a short fade haircut. He stands with a stoic, focused expression, looking upward to the left. His left arm rests casually on the female athlete's shoulder, while his right hand crosses his chest to adjust a navy blue Nike compression sleeve on his left arm. He is wearing the official Team USA Track & Field kit: a white Nike Aeroswift singlet with bold navy "USA" text and the Swoosh logo, paired with tight red Nike Aeroswift compression half-tights that reveal muscle contours. He wears white Nike Air Zoom Alphafly 3 shoes with neon accents and a thin gold necklace.
To his right stands a toned, fit female athlete with a deep skin tone and long dark wavy hair cascading over her shoulders. She leans slightly against the male subject while performing a standing quad stretch, balancing on her straight left leg and holding her bent right foot behind her. She wears a matching Team USA kit: a white Nike Aeroswift crop top with "USA" text and high-waisted red Nike racing briefs with the Swoosh on the hip. She wears a red compression sleeve on her right forearm, a watch, and white Nike ZoomX running shoes. Her expression is calm and confident.
The image must display hyper-realistic textures: the microscopic engineered mesh weave of the Nike Aeroswift fabric, the technical sheen of the spandex, and authentic skin texture showing visible pores, fine lines, natural hydration, and sweat. The clothing features realistic fabric folds and tension points corresponding to their poses. The color palette features a striking contrast between the cyan background and the red, white, and blue of the uniforms.
```

## Trompe-l'œil Keyhole Illusion with Living Eye

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

{
  "meta": {
    "aspect_ratio": "9:16",
    "quality": "ultra_photorealistic",
    "resolution": "8k",
    "camera": "iPhone 15 Pro Max (Main Lens)",
    "style": "Physical Trompe-l'œil, hyper-realistic photography, accurate scale and depth, no CGI look"
  },

  "scene": {
    "location": "Home interior, hallway/living room",
    "lighting": {
      "type": "Mixed lighting: Overhead recessed warm LED + soft ambient fill from side",
      "effect": "The overhead light creates a bright catchlight in the living eye and casts realistic, soft shadows inside the physical keyhole opening and under the book."
    },
    "background": "Neutral walls, white ceiling molding, dark window blinds in background left, framed art on right, all slightly out of focus"
  },

  "illusion_mechanics": {
    "concept": "Physical Die-Cut Hole & True Depth",
    "description": "The book cover is NOT a flat image. It has a physical, die-cut hole in the exact shape of the brass keyhole graphic. Through this physical opening, the viewer sees the actual, living right eye of the person holding the book.",
    "depth_perception": "The keyhole has visible thickness (the cardstock of the cover). The living eye is set back from the cover plane by a realistic distance (approx. 1-2cm), creating a genuine sense of looking *through* an object to the person behind it."
  },

  "subject": {
    "visible_features": "Long wavy honey-blonde hair with highlights, hands holding the book, school uniform.",
    "outfit": "Dark navy cable-knit cardigan, white button-up shirt, plaid tie (navy, grey, yellow stripes).",
    "the_eye": {
      "type": "Living human eye (anatomically accurate right eye)",
      "characteristics": "Wet sclera with subtle capillaries, complex iris pattern (hazel/brown), dilated pupil, visible blood vessels, realistic eyelashes.",
      "scale_and_proportion": "The eye is positioned and scaled precisely as if the book is pressed against the brow bone. It fills the keyhole naturally and anatomically correctly, without distortion.",
      "lighting_interaction": "The eye is lit by the room's light. A sharp, realistic catchlight from the overhead LED is visible on the cornea. The edges of the keyhole cast a soft, realistic shadow onto the skin and eyelid surrounding the eye."
    }
  },

  "prop_specifications": {
    "object": "Paperback book '{argument name="book title" default="THE HOUSEMAID"}' by Freida McFadden",
    "orientation": "Vertical, held flat in front of face. The image is a mirror selfie, so all text and the cover art are horizontally flipped.",
    "cover_details": {
      "material": "Matte cardstock with slight texture and wear on corners.",
      "background": "Deep blue with wood-grain texture.",
      "keyhole_frame": "The antique brass frame is printed on the cover, but the hole inside it is physically cut out.",
      "text": "All text is mirrored and legible: '{argument name="book title" default="THE HOUSEMAID"}' (Large, Yellow, Bold"
    }
  }
}
```

## Gothic K-Drama Fusion Illustration with Identity Preservation

> Sinematik, minimal afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded image strictly as an identity reference. Preserve 100% exact face match, facial structure, expressions, skin tone, hairstyle, and identity of every person visible in the reference image. Do not add or remove any person. Create a Gothic ✕ K-Drama fusion pose (for example: standing close with emotional tension, soft side glance under dramatic shadows, calm intimate posture) while keeping anatomy natural and elegant. Style the subjects in a blend of modern K-drama fashion and subtle gothic elements—clean coats, flowing silhouettes, muted dark fabrics, minimal metallic accents—without changing facial identity. Convert the scene into a premium illustration blended with clean vector art. Use refined linework, soft gradients, gentle shadow transitions, and selective high-contrast edges to balance romance and mystery. Lighting should be cinematic low-key with soft diffused highlights to keep faces tender yet dramatic. Color palette should blend romantic pastels (ivory, blush, soft blue) with gothic tones (charcoal, deep plum, midnight black). Background should be minimal and atmospheric—soft fog, abstract arches, or subtle architectural hints—simplified into layered vector forms. Render in ultra high resolution with crisp edges, emotional depth, and a polished fusion poster finish.",
  
  "negative_prompt": "face mismatch, identity change, extra person, missing person, horror gore, heavy gothic makeup, anime exaggeration, chibi, wrong facial features, wrong skin tone, bad anatomy, extra fingers, extra limbs, harsh noise, photorealism, low quality, blur, watermark, logo, text",
  
  "style": "gothic × K-drama fusion illustration + vector art",
  "mood": "romantic, mysterious, emotional",
  "lighting": "low-key cinematic with soft diffused highlights",
  "background": "minimal atmospheric gothic-romantic vector backdrop",
  "detail_level": "high but soft",
  "render_quality": "ultra high resolution, cinematic clarity",
  "composition": "same persons, intimate fusion pose",
  "reference_strength": 0.95
}
```

## Ultra Realistic Fashion Editorial with Identity and Scene Preservation

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "job_id": "photo_02_carry",
  "model": "nano-banana-pro",
  "task": "image_generation",
  "inputs": {
    "face_reference_image": "<YOUR_FACE_IMAGE>",
    "scene_reference_image": "<SCENE_IMAGE_2>"
  },
  "prompt": "Ultra realistic fashion editorial. EXACT SAME FACE as face_reference_image, identical identity. Recreate scene exactly from scene_reference_image: outdoor setting with tall mirrored panels reflecting trees. Muscular shirtless man standing, carrying the woman horizontally over shoulders. Woman wearing light fabric dress, arms and legs extended. Same pose, same proportions, same camera angle and framing. High-contrast, sharp details, cinematic fashion look.",
  "negative_prompt": "different face, identity drift, illustration, CGI, plastic skin, deformed body, extra limbs, wrong pose, wrong mirrors, text, watermark, blur",

    "aspect_ratio": "9:16",
    "resolution": { "width": 1024, "height": 1365 },
    "stylization": 0,
    "quality": "max",
    "lighting_match": "max",
    "composition_match": "max",

## Indian Saree Fashion Portraits (Two Prompts)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt 1:
"Create a beautiful young woman with long, wavy dark hair posing gracefully. She has a soft smile and is looking slightly away from the camera.
Outfit: She is wearing a modern, elegant sage green lehenga-sari set. The blouse is a sleeveless, V-neck cropped top featuring intricate silver sequins, horizontal pleating, and delicate beaded fringe at the hem. The skirt/drape is made of a shimmering, high-shine metallic silk or satin fabric in a matching light olive or pastel green.
Accessories: She is adorned with heavy traditional Indian jewelry, including large silver Chandbali earrings and a stack of matching bangles on one wrist.
Setting & Lighting: A minimalist indoor studio
setting with a clean off-white background. To the right, there is a lush vertical arrangement of white hydrangeas and roses. Soft, warm lighting creates a gentle glow, complemented by a decorative hanging crystal lamp or "jhumar" in the foreground. Photography Style: A high-resolution fashion
portrait with a shallow depth of field, soft shadows, and a clean, airy aesthetic. Use my uploaded image."

Prompt 2:
"Using my face, Subject: Young Indian/South Asian woman, "soft girl" aesthetic.
Skin: Warm undertone, "glass skin" makeup, high-gloss highlighter on cheekbones and nose, soft pink lips. 
Hair: Dark brown/black, loose waves, voluminous, messy but styled. 
Attire: Green saree (looks like organza or chiffon tissue fabric), black blouse with red/green embroidery on the hem, silver oxidized choker, silver jhumka earrings. 
Pose & Expression: Sitting on the floor, relaxed, leaning back, hand resting gently on the knee/saree. 
Head position: Chin tilted up, face turned slightly away from the camera toward the light source. Expression: Serene, peaceful, "main character energy," eyes half-closed or looking down/away. 
Environment & Props: 
Background: White/Cream wall, curtains with vertical floral vine embroidery. Furniture: Wooden cabinet/side table with a warm varnish finish. Props: Antique brass/metal narrow-neck vase, bouquet of orange and yellow roses + (fake/fabric flowers), stack of books (blue, white, grey spines), reading glasses, dark hanging tassel ornament on the drawer knob. 
Lighting & Atmosphere (Crucial): Type: Direct sunlight, Golden Hour, Sunset light. Effect: Chiaroscuro (strong contrast between light and dark). Shadows: The light is casting a "gobo" effect (shadow of a window grid or blinds) onto the wall and the subject.Vibe: Warm, nostalgic, cozy, aesthetic, vintage. 
Camera Settings & Tech Angle: Eye-level or slightly low angle. Focal Length: 35mm or 50mm (portrait but including environment). Aperture: f/2.8 or f/4 (Subject is sharp, background is slightly softened but readable).Style: Kodak Portra 400 film emulation (for that warm, grainy look)."
```

## Black and White Cinematic Motion Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra-realistic black-and-white cinematic portrait of a young man walking through a busy night-time city - use face reference exactly. He remains sharply in focus while the crowd around him blurs into dynamic motion, creating a striking sense of isolation, depth, and introspection. Soft urban lights form abstract bokeh streaks behind him, enhancing the moody atmosphere. His expression is intense and thoughtful, hair slightly tousled, wearing a dark coat layered over a loosely unbuttoned shirt. High-contrast lighting, shallow depth of field, dramatic motion blur, film-noir aesthetic, and clean poster-style framing with crisp 8K realism.
```

## High-Fashion Studio Collage with Identity Lock Prompt

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Create a realistic high-fashion studio photo collage featuring the same woman in six to eight different poses, using the uploaded face image as the exact facial reference (same facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, skin tone, and overall identity).  The woman has a slim and elegant body, natural proportions, pale to light skin tone, a delicate oval face, soft but sharp facial features, large expressive eyes, thin lips, and a calm, confident expression. Her hair is long, black, styled in a messy bun with loose strands falling naturally around the face.  Outfit: a tight black strapless mini dress, paired with sheer black thigh-high stockings, pointed-toe black stiletto heels with thin heels, and a formal black blazer worn in different ways across poses (fully worn, draped over one shoulder, hanging off one arm, or resting loosely on the arms). Accessories include a thin black choker necklace.  Poses included in the collage:  Standing front-facing full body pose, neutral expression, blazer hanging off the arms.  Standing back-facing pose with the head turned toward the camera, blazer worn on the shoulders, legs straight.  Squatting or semi-crouching pose with one leg forward, sharp gaze toward the camera.  Seated pose on a black chair with legs crossed, blazer off one shoulder, elegant and composed expression.  Seated low pose with knees bent, body leaning slightly forward, blazer worn on one arm only.  Half-body portrait facing the camera, shoulders relaxed, clean editorial look.  Environment: clean white seamless studio background with soft high-key lighting. Large softbox light placed in front and slightly to the side, creating soft shadows beneath the body. Even lighting that clearly shows fabric texture, skin detail, and shoe shine.  Photography style: high-end fashion editorial, professional studio photography, ultra high-definition, sharp focus, natural skin texture, realistic shadows, no lens distortion, accurate anatomy and proportions.  Composition: vertical fashion collage layout with evenly spaced images.  Aspect ratio: 9:16."
```

## High-Fashion Double Exposure Portrait with Reflection

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "version": "1.1",
  "description": "Create a high-fashion editorial double exposure portrait. The image should feature two distinct expressions creating a narrative contrast, bathed in a specific warm golden light flare against a cool background, with a mirrored reflection below.",
  "variables": {
    "PRIMARY_EXPRESSION": {
      "type": "string",
      "value": "Intense, direct, unyielding gaze into the camera",
      "description": "The grounded, sharp expression of the subject in the immediate foreground."
    },
    "SECONDARY_EXPRESSION": {
      "type": "string",
      "value": "A distant, melancholic look towards the left, as if lost in a fading memory or dream",
      "description": "The narrative-driven, emotive expression for the translucent, overlaid image that suggests inner thought or past versus present."
    }
  },
  "steps": [
    {
      "name": "NARRATIVE_DOUBLE_EXPOSURE",
      "goal": "Layer two images to create an emotional story, using a specific color palette for contrast.",
      "instructions": "Use the subject from the input image to create a cinematic double exposure.\
\
1.  **Primary Layer (The Present):** A sharp, high-definition portrait. The subject has the {argument name="primary expression" default="Intense, direct, unyielding gaze into the camera"}. The lighting is neutral to cool studio light, highlighting skin texture and silver accessories cleanly.\
2.  **Secondary Layer (The Inner World/Memory):** A translucent overlay, slightly offset. The subject has the {argument name="secondary expression" default="A distant, melancholic look towards the left, as if lost in a fading memory or dream"}. This layer must be heavily influenced by a **Warm Golden-Amber Editorial Light Leak** (like the golden hour filter in image_1). This warm tone should wash over the face and hair, creating a dreamlike, almost sepia-toned contrast against the cooler primary layer."
    },
    {
      "name": "MIRRORED_COMPOSITION",
      "goal": "Replicate the specific posing and reflective surface from the reference.",
      "instructions": "The subject is posed leaning forward with hands crossed over a highly polished, dark reflective surface. The primary layer (cool tones, direct gaze) must be perfectly mirrored below, adding symmetry and grounding the ethereal double exposure above."
    },
    {
      "name": "EDITORIAL_ATMOSPHERE",
      "goal": "Finalize the lighting and mood to match a high-end magazine aesthetic.",
      "instructions": "The background should be a smooth, muted cool grey/blue gradient studio wall, providing a clean backdrop that makes the warm golden flare pop. The overall feel should be moody, cinematic, and luxurious, capturing a frozen moment of introspection."
    }
  ],
  "final_goal": "Produce a single, high-fashion photographic still using double exposure. It must feature the subject with a sharp, direct gaze contrasted with a translucent, story-filled golden-amber memory overlay, all reflected in a polished surface beneath."
}
```

{
  "description": "Candid lifestyle image of a model wearing the exact same {argument name="dress color" default="olive"} short dress — identical neckline, waist seam, and fit. The outfit is styled casually with a chunky off-white knit cardigan draped naturally over the shoulders or worn open, paired with clean white sneakers. The scene is outdoors in a cozy, casual setting such as a park bench, café terrace, or shaded sidewalk area. The photo should feel spontaneous, like a real moment captured mid-day.",
  
  "appearance": "beautiful white american model. Hair remains natural and loose, gently tousled by outdoor air. No heavy makeup — just minimal, natural tones with visible skin texture. Expression relaxed, calm, and content.",
  
  "pose": "Natural candid moment — the model could be walking with a coffee cup, sitting casually on a park bench, or standing while adjusting her cardigan. The pose should appear effortless and unscripted, avoiding stiffness or symmetry. Capture a sense of motion or relaxed stillness.",

  "camera": "Full-body vertical shot or mid-length candid framing, slightly off-center as if taken by a friend. Eye-level angle with mild depth blur for realism. Composition should have breathing space around the model for a relaxed visual flow.",
  
  "style": "Realistic lifestyle photograph with a cozy, relatable tone. Editorial-level sharpness but emotionally warm and grounded. Subtle film-like warmth may be added to lighting balance.",
  
  "focus": "Highlight the olive dress with cardigan layering, natural pose, and lifestyle context. Emphasize realism, motion, and candid imperfection for authenticity.",

Edit this photo without altering the face. create a photorealistic potrait of 
A young woman with fair, smooth skin and naturally soft pink lips, with dark brown hair styled with bangs.  She is sitting in a relaxed pose on a toilet. She wears a white button-up shirt, slightly open at the collar, with sleeves rolled up to the elbows, paired with black suspenders and wearing a black pant.

Use the original face 100% exactly as shown in the provided reference photo, with no alterations.
```

The background remains uniform but subtly graded, adding depth without distraction. ## High-Fashion Monochrome Vector Illustration Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded image strictly as an identity reference. Preserve 100% exact face match, facial structure, expressions, skin tone (translated accurately into grayscale), hairstyle, and identity of every person visible in the reference image. Do not add or remove any person. Create a new high-fashion editorial pose (for example: confident upright stance, slight profile angle, strong posture, composed expressions) while keeping anatomy natural and elegant. Convert the scene into a pure black-and-white illustration blended with refined vector art. Use crisp black ink outlines, smooth grayscale shading, and controlled contrast to define facial structure and form. Skin tones should translate naturally into grayscale values without losing identity. Clothing should remain similar in type but rendered in monochrome with clear vector edges, folds, and tonal separation. Lighting should be dramatic and directional, emphasizing facial features and silhouettes through light–shadow contrast. Redesign the background into a minimal editorial vector backdrop using negative space, gradients in grayscale, or subtle geometric balance. Render in ultra high resolution with sharp edges, clean layers, and a sophisticated magazine-style black-and-white finish.",
  
  "negative_prompt": "face mismatch, identity change, extra person, missing person, wrong facial features, bad anatomy, extra fingers, extra limbs, color elements, photorealism, heavy textures, painterly strokes, low quality, blur, noise, grain, watermark, logo, text",
  
  "style": "black and white editorial illustration + vector art",
  "mood": "bold, elegant, high-fashion",
  "lighting": "dramatic directional monochrome lighting",
  "background": "minimal grayscale vector backdrop",
  "detail_level": "high and refined",
  "render_quality": "ultra high resolution, editorial clarity",
  "composition": "same persons, new editorial pose, monochrome palette",
  "reference_strength": 0.8
}
```

## High-End Sports Photography: Competitive Road Cyclist

> Sinematik, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An attractive blonde woman engaged in competitive road cycling, captured mid-motion at high speed. She is wearing a streamlined cycling helmet and a performance sports bra with a deep neckline, designed for professional racing—athletic, functional, and stylish. Her build is fit and strong, with defined but natural musculature. She is riding a modern racing bicycle, hands gripping the drop handlebars, body leaned forward in an aerodynamic stance. The bicycle frame, wheels, chain, and pedals are clearly visible, integrated naturally into the composition. The shot is a close, dynamic action shot that shows both the cyclist and most of the bicycle, emphasizing fluid motion. Subtle motion blur in the background and wheels conveys speed, while her face and upper body remain sharp. Her expression is focused and determined, eyes forward, jaw set with competitive intensity. Lighting is natural daylight, slightly directional, highlighting muscle tone and contours without harsh contrast. Color palette is clean and realistic: neutral road tones, modern sportswear colors, polished bike materials. The overall style is high-end sports photography, realistic and energetic, not cinematic-heavy. Camera angle is slightly low and forward-facing, enhancing power and momentum. Shallow depth of field isolates the subject while keeping the bicycle clearly readable. Crisp detail, realistic textures, professional athletic aesthetic. Optional Style Modifiers professional sports photography high shutter speed dynamic motion blur natural daylight realistic skin tones modern athletic branding (no logos)
```

## Precise Pose and Composition Replication Prompt

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
I. Core Goals and Strong Constraints
1. Precise Pose Replication: Use Image 2 as the sole reference for the pose, and perform a “point-by-point alignment” replication of the full-body pose of the person in Image 1 (ensuring consistency in the orientation and curvature of the head, neck, shoulders, chest, hips, knees, ankles, arms, wrists, and fingers).
2. Consistent Perspective and Lens: The camera position, shooting angle, lens height, pitch angle, yaw angle, perspective relationship, and depth-of-field effect must be consistent with Image 2.
3. Consistent Composition: The position, proportion, negative space ratio, and cropping method (whether feet/head/hands are cropped) of the person in the frame must be consistent with Image 2.
4. Consistent Head and Gaze: The head tilt angle, chin lift/tuck degree, and gaze direction must be consistent with Image 2.
5. Appearance Prohibition: Strictly retain the identity features and style of the person in Image 1 (facial features, hairstyle, hair color, skin tone, clothing style and color, material, accessories, texture, body proportion, age temperament, art style/rendering style). Only change the pose and lens composition to match Image 2.

II. Execution Method and Calibration Requirements
1. Alignment Method: Use the skeleton of the person in Image 2 as the “target skeleton,” and “redirect” the skeleton of the person in Image 1 to the target skeleton; the limb angles, center of gravity position, and torso twist magnitude must match.
2. Key Point Priority: First match the center of gravity and pelvic orientation, then match the torso rotation and shoulder line, then match the extremities (hands, feet), and finally fine-tune finger and expression details.
3. Allowable Range of Variation: The error in pose and lens should be as close to zero as possible; if unavoidable, the error must be extremely small and not visually obvious.
4. Prohibitions: Do not add or delete objects; do not change clothing styling or details; do not change the character's style; do not change the background style leading to compositional changes; do not transfer the appearance features of the person in Image 2 to the person in Image 1.
```

## Sultry Korean Beauty Culinary Portrait Prompt

> Sinematik, retro yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A sultry Korean beauty with porcelain skin and captivating dark eyes creates an intimate culinary moment, photographed in the style of a high-end lifestyle magazine editorial. She leans gracefully over a rustic wooden table, her silky black hair cascading as she delicately tastes from an elegant ceramic spoon, steam from a sophisticated white ceramic pot creating dreamy atmosphere around her sensual silhouette.

Shot in cinematic flat lay composition from above, the scene captures multiple angles of this enchanting moment - her full glossy lips parting slightly as she savors the taste, her slender fingers with perfectly manicured nails gripping the spoon, and close-up details of the artfully plated dish in fine porcelain bowls. The lighting mimics golden hour warmth streaming through sheer curtains, creating soft rim lighting that accentuates her elegant collarbone and the gentle curve of her shoulders beneath a flowing silk off-shoulder blouse.

The aesthetic channels Annie Leibovitz's intimate portraiture style with film photography grain, slightly desaturated tones for nostalgic warmth, and scattered handwritten recipe cards, vintage brass utensils, and fresh herbs creating an organic scrapbook composition. Captured on Canon EOS R5 with 85mm f/1.2 lens, shallow depth of field isolates her alluring expression while the steaming broth and scattered ingredients create layers of visual storytelling around this captivating culinary goddess.. A stylish handwritten signature Willy is elegantly and small letters placed at the Bottom Right corner.#prompt #koreanfood #korean #woman #portrait #koreabeauty
```

## High-Fashion Studio Photo Collage Prompt

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic high-fashion studio photo collage featuring the same woman in six to eight different poses, using the uploaded face image as the exact facial reference (same facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, skin tone, and overall identity).

The woman has a slim and elegant body, natural proportions, pale to light skin tone, a delicate oval face, soft but sharp facial features, large expressive eyes, thin lips, and a calm, confident expression. Her hair is long, black, styled in a messy bun with loose strands falling naturally around the face.

Outfit: a tight black strapless mini dress, paired with sheer black thigh-high stockings, pointed-toe black stiletto heels with thin heels, and a formal black blazer worn in different ways across poses (fully worn, draped over one shoulder, hanging off one arm, or resting loosely on the arms). Accessories include a thin black choker necklace.

Poses included in the collage:

Standing front-facing full body pose, neutral expression, blazer hanging off the arms.

Standing back-facing pose with the head turned toward the camera, blazer worn on the shoulders, legs straight.

Squatting or semi-crouching pose with one leg forward, sharp gaze toward the camera.

Seated pose on a black chair with legs crossed, blazer off one shoulder, elegant and composed expression.

Seated low pose with knees bent, body leaning slightly forward, blazer worn on one arm only.

Half-body portrait facing the camera, shoulders relaxed, clean editorial look.

Environment: clean white seamless studio background with soft high-key lighting. Large softbox light placed in front and slightly to the side, creating soft shadows beneath the body. Even lighting that clearly shows fabric texture, skin detail, and shoe shine.

Photography style: high-end fashion editorial, professional studio photography, ultra high-definition, sharp focus, natural skin texture, realistic shadows, no lens distortion, accurate anatomy and proportions.

Composition: vertical fashion collage layout with evenly spaced images.

Aspect ratio: 9:16.
```

## Book Recommendation Engine to Automaton Prompt

> Minimal, fütüristik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instructions> Act as an elite book recommendation engine + museum-miniature kinetic-sculpture designer. INPUT: The user provides (a) a book title/author OR (b) an image of a book/object inspired by a book. Step 1 — Vibe analysis (internal only; do NOT output): Infer tone, pacing, world rules, character dynamics, thematic engine, sensory palette, and emotional aftertaste. Step 2 — Selection (internal only; do NOT output): Select 4 under-the-radar books with the closest vibe match (avoid obvious top-10 staples unless the input is already obscure). Step 3 — Output (ONLY output this; no extra commentary): Output ONE single image-generation prompt describing a 2x2 grid (four equal panels, thin clean dividers). Each panel is a miniature set built into/onto a classic book-object and includes a working kinetic sculpture/automaton that expresses that book’s core story through motion. No readable paragraphs. Under each panel: engraved plate “Title (Year)”, no extra text.
Default Template: 2x2 grid; each panel is a whittled wooden tabletop automaton mounted on a book-shaped base, with characters and elements from 4 random sci-fi books driven by a side crank with exposed cams and linkages. Style: Premium studio macro product photography, crisp micro-scratches on brass pins, clean wood joinery, neutral softbox lighting, medium shallow depth of field, 50mm perspective, seamless background in warm light gray.</instructions>
```

## Cinematic Editorial Fantasy Portrait Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a cinematic ultra-real editorial fashion photograph using my uploaded reference image. DO NOT CHANGE THE FACE.
The young man’s face must remain 100% identical to the reference — same facial structure, proportions, expression, and identity.

SKIN & REALISM

Ultra-real human skin with even tone and natural micro-variation. Visible pores, fine lines, tiny imperfections, and a subtle daylight sheen only.
Absolutely NO blush, no beauty filter, no plastic smoothing, no CGI look, no oversharpening.

SCENE & PROPS

A vast wildflower field filled with pink, lilac, and butter-yellow blooms, stretching toward low rolling hills beneath a big, clean open sky.
A giant pastel-blue bird with layered, slightly iridescent feathers. The bird wears slender reins and a simple dark saddle — minimal, realistic, no logos, no text, no branding.
Foreground flowers create soft natural bokeh, with a few drifting petals near the lens for depth and atmosphere.

WARDROBE (MODERN, MINIMAL, STREETWEAR)

A clean, premium oversized modern T-shirt in soft off-white or muted neutral tone, structured yet relaxed, matte fabric with natural folds.
Baggy straight-leg jeans, high-quality denim in washed light blue or soft grey, sitting naturally on the waist with realistic creasing and weight.
Polished dark leather boots or minimalist sneakers (no logos), understated and contemporary.
One slim signet ring only — no additional accessories.

POSE & MOOD

He is seated upright on the giant bird’s back, shoulders relaxed, posture confident yet calm. Chin gently elevated.
Expression is serene and self-assured, eyes gazing toward the horizon.
The T-shirt and denim move subtly with the breeze, natural fabric motion.

LIGHTING (GOLDEN HOUR)

Warm late-afternoon sunlight (~6000K) coming from camera-left/back, creating gentle rim highlights along his face, clothing edges, and the bird’s feathers.
Soft fill light from the opposite side keeps skin fully natural and realistic.
Colors are rich but never oversaturated.

CAMERA / OPTICS / DOF

Shot on a real full-frame camera.
50mm lens at f/2.8, ISO 100–200, shutter speed 1/500–1/1000s to freeze subtle feather motion.
Medium depth of field: subject and nearby feathers sharp, distant hills and far flowers softly blurred.
Vertical composition with a low horizon to emphasize the sky.
An elegant S-curve formed by the bird’s tail guides the viewer’s eye through the frame.

STYLE & OUTPUT

Pinterest-style cinematic editorial grading.
Ultra-photorealistic, grounded fantasy realism.
8K resolution.
```

## Cinematic Night Urban Portrait Prompt

> Sinematik, fütüristik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-resolution portrait, HD, 4K quality.
Use the uploaded photo as the reference and preserve all real facial features exactly — facial structure, proportions, expression, skin tone, and overall appearance without any alterations.
A woman [reference face in the photo] with slightly damp clothing. Her expression is dreamy, looking up toward the camera with a soft gaze.
Clothing:
She is wearing an oversized slate grey/teal hoodie, slightly damp from the weather.Neon signs in red and yellow glow above the street.
Reflections:
Bright red neon and brake‑light reflections dominate the shiny wet pavement, contrasting with the cool blue/grey ambient tones.
Lighting:
Cinematic night lighting: strong, dramatic neon illumination with high contrast between warm red/yellow lights and cool ambient shadows.
Composition:
Vertical portrait, medium shot. The subject stands on the right side, framed by the textured poster wall and the blurred wet street on the left.
Depth of field:
Shallow depth of field with pronounced bokeh keeps the background softly blurred while keeping the character sharply in focus.Environment — Night Urban Scene: A busy city street at night (Tokyo / Seoul / Hong Kong vibe). Weather: Light rain or drizzle, visible through reflections on the wet pavement and faint water particles in the air. Foreground: She leans against a wall densely covered in layered posters, flyers, and stickers, providing a gritty urban texture on the right side of the frame. Background – Street: A wet, reflective street with several distant cars visible only through their vivid red brake lights.
```

## Vector Art Style Transformation Prompt

> Minimal illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded image as a strict identity reference only. Preserve 100% exact face match, facial structure, expressions, skin tone, hairstyle, and identity of every person visible in the reference image. Do not add or remove any person. While keeping identity intact, change the pose to a new natural and visually appealing pose suitable for illustration (for example: slight body turn, relaxed stance, different hand placement, or subtle interaction between people). Update the color palette to a fresh, modern scheme with harmonious tones, such as pastel gradients, warm–cool contrast, or bold contemporary colors, while keeping skin tones realistic and consistent. Convert the scene into a high-quality illustration blended with clean vector art style. Use crisp outlines, smooth curves, flat-to-semi-flat color fills, and soft gradient accents. Clothing design should remain similar in type but recolored to match the new palette, with clean vector shapes and refined edges. Lighting should be soft and even, optimized for illustration and vector clarity. Background should be redesigned into a minimal, stylish vector backdrop using gradients or abstract shapes that complement the new pose and colors. Render in ultra high resolution with sharp edges, clean layers, and a professional illustration + vector finish.",
  
  "negative_prompt": "face mismatch, identity change, extra person, missing person, wrong facial features, wrong skin tone, bad anatomy, extra fingers, extra limbs, distorted hands, photorealism, rough textures, painterly brush strokes, dull colors, low quality, blur, noise, grain, watermark, logo, text",
  
  "style": "illustration + vector art",
  "mood": "modern, fresh, stylish",
  "lighting": "soft even lighting",
  "background": "minimal vector background with abstract shapes or gradients",
  "detail_level": "high but clean",
  "render_quality": "ultra high resolution, crisp vector clarity",
  "composition": "same persons, new pose, updated colors",
  "reference_strength": 0.85
}
```

## High-Fashion Black Elegance Photo Collage Prompt

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic high-fashion studio photo collage featuring the same woman in six to eight different poses, using the uploaded face image as the exact facial reference (same facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, skin tone, and overall identity).

The woman has a slim and elegant body, natural proportions, pale to light skin tone, a delicate oval face, soft but sharp facial features, large expressive eyes, thin lips, and a calm, confident expression. Her hair is long, black, styled in a messy bun with loose strands falling naturally around the face.

Outfit: a tight black strapless mini dress, paired with sheer black thigh-high stockings, pointed-toe black stiletto heels with thin heels, and a formal black blazer worn in different ways across poses (fully worn, draped over one shoulder, hanging off one arm, or resting loosely on the arms). Accessories include a thin black choker necklace.

Poses included in the collage:

Standing front-facing full body pose, neutral expression, blazer hanging off the arms.

Standing back-facing pose with the head turned toward the camera, blazer worn on the shoulders, legs straight.

Squatting or semi-crouching pose with one leg forward, sharp gaze toward the camera.

Seated pose on a black chair with legs crossed, blazer off one shoulder, elegant and composed expression.

Seated low pose with knees bent, body leaning slightly forward, blazer worn on one arm only.

Half-body portrait facing the camera, shoulders relaxed, clean editorial look.

Environment: clean white seamless studio background with soft high-key lighting. Large softbox light placed in front and slightly to the side, creating soft shadows beneath the body. Even lighting that clearly shows fabric texture, skin detail, and shoe shine.

Photography style: high-end fashion editorial, professional studio photography, ultra high high-definition, sharp focus, natural skin texture, realistic shadows, no lens distortion, accurate anatomy and proportions.

Composition: vertical fashion collage layout with evenly spaced images.

Aspect ratio: 9:16.
```

## Charcoal and Chalk Fashion Editorial Prompt (Identity Preserve)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "input_type": "image_to_image",
  "target_image": "UPLOAD_IMAGE_HERE",
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded reference image to preserve the subject’s exact facial identity, proportions, hair, and skin tone with zero alteration. Reimagine the portrait as a high-end charcoal and chalk fashion editorial artwork. The subject may be a man or a woman; adapt styling and pose accordingly while maintaining strength, confidence, and elegance. Render the face with highly realistic charcoal detailing—rich blacks, soft greys, and precise facial structure—while highlights on cheekbones, jawline, collarbones, and hair are created using subtle white or warm chalk accents. Clothing is suggested through expressive charcoal strokes and tonal contrast, implying Western luxury silhouettes without detailed textures. Pose is strong and editorial, calm yet commanding. Background is minimal and atmospheric, composed of smudged charcoal gradients or textured paper grain. Lighting is dramatic and directional, sculpting the subject through contrast rather than color. Overall mood is bold, raw, timeless, and powerful—fashion editorial meets fine-art charcoal portrait.",
  "style": "charcoal and chalk fashion editorial",
  "preserve_identity": true,
  "preserve_face": true,
  "style_strength": 0.9,
  "quality": "16K",
  "negative_prompt": "color painting, watercolor wash, ink illustration, digital art look, cartoon, anime, photorealism, smooth airbrush, explicit content, cluttered background"
}
```

Reimagine the portrait as a high-end ink-wash fashion editorial artwork. The subject may be a man or a woman; adapt styling and pose accordingly while maintaining confidence and authority. Render the face with precise realism using controlled ink lines and soft wash shading, while the body, clothing, and background are expressed through expressive brush ink, diluted washes, and negative space. Clothing appears as Western luxury couture suggested through flowing ink strokes and tonal contrast rather than detailed fabric texture. Pose is strong and editorial, calm yet dominant. Background remains minimal—soft ink gradients, abstract brush fades, or blank paper space. Lighting is dramatic and directional, created through ink contrast and wash density. Overall mood is modern Western luxury, artistic power, restraint, and sophistication, resembling a premium fashion sketch elevated to gallery art.",
  "style": "ink-wash fashion editorial illustration",
  "preserve_identity": true,
  "preserve_face": true,
  "style_strength": 0.85,
  "quality": "16K",
  "negative_prompt": "watercolor pastel look, oil paint, acrylic texture, digital illustration, cartoon, anime, harsh color fills, photorealism, explicit content, cluttered background"
}

## Masterpiece Painting Reimagined as a Charcuterie Platter

> Illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Act as a world class food expert and artist. 

Input A: Masterpiece painting (e.g., {argument name="painting name" default="Mona Lisa"}, Starry Night, The Scream).

 Analyze composition, colors, mood, elements. Pick the right food ingredients relevant to the region that can be used in the painting. 

Goal: Large charcuterie platter replicating the painting using cured meats, cheeses, fruits, nuts, and other ingredients relevant to that region. 
Rules: decide which ingredients should be used for each part, so the painting looks as realistic as possible but is mainly made out of food. 

2x2 grid: Panel 1: Original painting reference; Panel 2: Ingredient map; Panel 3: Overhead build; Panel 4: Final board with wine pairing.
Output: 1 x 1:1 image, rustic gourmet photography.
```

## Futuristic Nature and Technology Landscape Portrait

> Sinematik, fütüristik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A middle-aged South Asian man standing confidently on a cliff edge in a three-quarter profile pose, one foot slightly forward, hands relaxed at his sides, gazing toward the distant horizon. He wears a modern outdoor jacket in deep charcoal, rugged trekking pants, and sturdy hiking boots, with no cap, his hair subtly moving in the wind. The background follows a futuristic nature–meets–technology theme: a vast valley at sunrise filled with rolling mist, dense forests, and soft clouds, illuminated by glowing teal and golden sky gradients. In the distance, the number “2026” appears as a monumental symbol formed from translucent light, green vegetation, and flowing energy-like water streams, integrated into floating terraces rather than a traditional waterfall. Cinematic wide-angle composition, ultra-detailed, high dynamic range lighting, inspirational and forward-looking mood, peaceful yet powerful atmosphere symbolizing progress, vision, and new beginnings.
```

## High-Aesthetic 2026 Vision Board Collage

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-aesthetic 2026 vision board collage combining beautiful lifestyle photos with visible inspirational text and affirmations.
Use the uploaded face image as the main character in several frames, preserving facial identity accurately.

The vision board represents an ideal 2026 life: success, peace, love, confidence, freedom, abundance, and self-alignment.
Editorial yet dreamy visuals, soft luxury lifestyle, intentional living.

IMPORTANT:
Text is a key element.
Short phrases, affirmations, and words must be visibly integrated into the collage.
Typography should feel aesthetic, handwritten, serif, or clean minimal fonts.
Text should feel emotional, motivational, and personal — not corporate.

Examples of text to include (can appear across different frames):
“2026”
“everything works out”
“soft life, strong mind”
“aligned”
“chosen”
“abundance”
“calm success”
“romanticize your life”
“she believed herself”
“slow mornings”
“healthy, wealthy, loved”
“this is my year”

Scenes & visuals to include:

- Portrait of the woman in soft natural light, calm confident expression, editorial but intimate.
- Cozy morning scene: sunlight through curtains, coffee, notebook, peaceful atmosphere.
- Career / purpose scene: working in a beautiful cafe or modern space, laptop open, focused calm energy.
- Travel or freedom scene: window, balcony, city view, or open space, cinematic framing.
- Love & connection: subtle romantic imagery (hands, closeness, warmth), tasteful and minimal.
- Luxury details: coffee cups, jewelry, bags, flowers, neutral tones.
- Self-care & health: walking, stretching, slow movement, wellness aesthetic.

Visual style:
Soft editorial lighting, neutral and warm color palette (beige, cream, taupe, warm whites),
film look, subtle grain, gentle contrast, elegant composition.

Text placement:
Some text handwritten over images,
some typed cleanly,
some layered like a journal or scrapbook.
Text should feel organic and emotionally intentional, not perfectly aligned.

Mood & vibe:
Manifestation energy, feminine calm power, romanticized life, clarity, confidence, peace.
Beautiful photos first, meaningful words second — balanced and aesthetic.

High realism, natural skin texture, not AI-looking.

Aspect ratio: vertical 16:9
```

Club vibe, energetic, stylish, clean composition. No text/logos/watermarks.",

        },

          "festive, sexy, high-fashion party look",

        ]
      },

}

## High-Fidelity Skin Texture Portrait of a Young Doctor

> Lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 4:5 aspect ratio). A raw, ultra-high-resolution portrait taken with an iPhone 15 Pro Max (24mm lens) in a luxury day-lit clinic.

Subject: A young female doctor (early 20s).
*   Skin Physics (The Upgrade): The direct sunlight hits her face from the side. We can see "High-Fidelity Skin Texture": distinct pores on her nose and cheeks, fine "peach fuzz" (vellus hair) on her jawline backlit by the sun, and a realistic oily sheen on her T-zone and forehead. It is NOT matte; it looks like hydrated, living human skin.
*   Hair: "Chestnut Brown" hair. Individual strands are visible and backlit, creating a natural halo. Some flyaways stick up (static), breaking the perfect silhouette.
*   Expression: She leans against the glass, smiling genuinely (eyes crinkling, showing crow's feet hints) which proves it's a real expression, not a mask.

Outfit (Material Contrast):
*   White Lab Coat: We see the heavy weave of the cotton drill fabric. It has sharp creases at the elbow where her arm is bent.
*   Red silk bra with black lace trim.
*   Gold Necklace: Glints brightly (specular highlight) in the sun.

Environment & Lighting:
*   Lighting: "Hard Directional Daylight". The sun is bright and crisp. It creates a defined shadow of her eyelashes on her cheekbones and a shadow of her body on the glass she is leaning against.
*   Background (SHARP): A modern glass-walled clinic. We see reflections of the room on the glass she is leaning against. A white orchid is sharp in the background. The image is crisp, with no artificial smoothing.
```

## Outdoor Christmas Decoration from Input Image

> Lüks manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[AI Instruction: Exterior Theme Expansion]
Analyze the provided **[Input Image]**.
Theme Extraction: Identify the main subject, color palette, and specific motifs (e.g., stars, candy canes, specific characters, elegant gold style, cute pop style, etc.) from the [Input Image].
Scale Up: Expand these elements into large-scale outdoor Christmas decorations and lighting installations.
[Scene and Setting]
A festive landscape photo taken at night with a wide-angle view of a house's front yard lavishly decorated for Christmas. The ground and roof are covered with fresh snow.
[Main Features (Based on Input Image)]

Large-Scale Installation: Convert the main subject of the [Input Image] into a large, illuminated outdoor decoration placed in the center of the yard (e.g., a giant glowing wireframe object, inflatable decoration, a large wooden cutout illuminated by spotlights, etc.).
[Auxiliary Decorations (Matching Input Style)]
Lighting: The color of the LED string lights covering trees, shrubs, and the exterior walls of the house is determined based on the main colors of the [Input Image].
Accents: Garlands on porch railings, door wreaths, and path markers use motifs and colors directly extracted from the [Input Image] (e.g., if the input image has a red ribbon, a giant weatherproof red ribbon is used outdoors).
[Atmosphere]
Magically glowing and impressive. The entire scene has a unified feel because all elements are based on the single style guide of the [Input Image].
```

## Watercolor Fashion Editorial Identity Preservation

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "input_type": "image_to_image",
  "target_image": "UPLOAD_IMAGE_HERE",
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded reference image to preserve the woman’s exact facial identity, proportions, and skin tone with zero alteration. Transform the scene into a high-end watercolor-style Western fashion editorial portrait that feels minimalist, sensual, and powerful. Depict a young South Asian woman in her early 20s with a light brown complexion and a curvy, confident silhouette. Her natural curly dark hair flows freely with a soft, organic watercolor texture. Makeup appears editorial-clean in watercolor form: luminous skin washes, delicately sculpted cheekbones, softly diffused smoky eyes, and muted glossy lips rendered through gentle pigment layering. She wears a nude, champagne, or sand-toned satin or silk Western couture ensemble, interpreted through translucent watercolor washes and fluid brushwork—elegant, tasteful, and refined. Pose shows strong Western editorial confidence: weight shifted onto one leg, hips relaxed, one arm resting lightly, chin subtly lifted, expression calm, self-assured, and dominant through presence. Background is a solid monochrome watercolor wash in warm taupe, muted beige, or soft stone grey, with no props or distractions. Lighting is translated into watercolor through soft tonal transitions, gentle highlights on cheekbones and collarbones, and smooth shadow gradients. Overall mood is Western luxury, restrained sensuality, confidence, and modern femininity, rendered as a refined watercolor fashion illustration, not photorealistic and not digital.",
  "style": "high-end watercolor fashion illustration",
  "preserve_identity": true,
  "preserve_face": true,
  "style_strength": 0.8,
  "quality": "16K",
  "negative_prompt": "photorealistic, oil painting, acrylic texture, digital illustration, harsh outlines, cartoon style, anime, explicit content, exaggerated anatomy, cluttered background"
}

## High-Fashion Red Carpet Portrait with Reference Image Lock Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[reference_image_instructions]
face = "Use 100% face copy of the uploaded photo as reference, don’t alter her face. Use 100% of her facial features."
note = "The face must remain exactly identical to the reference image; only the body, clothing, hair, makeup, pose, and environment are to be transformed according to the description."

[prompt]
description = """
Create a full-body, high-fashion portrait of an incredibly elegant woman, wearing a form-fitting, body-con mermaid gown crafted from shimmering pale blush pink micro-sequined fabric. The dress features a dramatically plunging deep V-neckline and a high slit running up the left thigh, revealing a hint of her leg. The bodice is heavily embellished with intricate cascades of large, brilliant-cut rhinestones and silver crystal beadwork, creating a structured, harness-like effect that tapers down her small waist with delicate dangling jewel chains.

She is dramatically draped over her shoulders and down to the floor with an enormous, opulent floor-length ivory faux fur coat. The coat is worn off the shoulders, exposing the straps of the gown, and its plush texture and volume create a soft, majestic contrast to the shimmering dress. Her footwear consists of sleek silver metallic ankle-strap stiletto sandals. Accessories are minimal but exquisite: large, dangling teardrop diamond chandelier earrings.

Hairstyle & Makeup

Her hair is styled in flawless, high-volume classic Hollywood finger waves, deep side-parted and cascading over one shoulder, dyed a sophisticated {argument name=\"hair color\" default=\"ash-blonde/light caramel\"} hue. Her makeup is flawless and glamorous: perfectly sculpted cheekbones with soft coral blush, defined arched eyebrows, a subtle smoky cat-eye featuring winged eyeliner and voluminous lashes, and a matte lip color in a sophisticated terracotta nude shade.

Pose & Expression

She stands in a powerful and poised red carpet pose, hands positioned firmly on her hips, pushing the elbows slightly out to emphasize her small waistline and the dramatic draping of the fur coat. Her posture is erect and confident. Her expression is refined, with a subtle, knowing half-smile and a direct, penetrating gaze that locks onto the viewer.

Background & Lighting

The background is an extremely luxurious, warm-toned interior, resembling a grand hotel ballroom entrance or a magnificent hallway. Architectural details include tall, ornate golden/bronze trim and paneling set against walls of soft beige or cream. The floor is covered in glossy, patterned beige and brown marble tiles. Lighting is soft, warm, and cinematic ambient light (suggesting overhead chandeliers or sconces), casting a gentle, flattering glow with minimal harsh shadows, highlighting the shimmer of the sequins and the softness of the fur.

Camera & Photography Style

Full-body portrait shot. Ultra-realistic professional studio photograph. Shot with a high-resolution DSLR (e.g., Canon EOS R5 or Sony A1) and a fast prime lens
```

## Peter Lindbergh Style Black and White Editorial Portrait

```
A cinematic, ultra-realistic black-and-white portrait of a man for a high-fashion editorial project, capturing a moment of ecstatic release and impeccable chic: a mid-shot taken with an 85 mm lens at f/4 from chest level in a minimalist studio, where a seamless, absolute black void creates dramatic chiaroscuro and an atmosphere that is intimate, sensual, and powerfully calm. Hyper-realistic natural skin texture is emphasized by dramatic lighting without any retouching, while a relaxed three-quarter profile pose with one hand casually in a trouser pocket and the head slightly tilted back toward the light conveys ecstatic serenity, quiet confidence, and deep sensuality. He wears an oversized white men's shirt, deeply unbuttoned and loosely draped with sleeves rolled to the elbows, paired with simple dark tailored trousers. A single strong light source pulls the figure from the darkness, illuminating the contours of the face, neck, and the folds of the shirt with extreme contrast, sculptural clarity, and a Vogue-level high-fashion aesthetic reminiscent of Peter Lindbergh an 8K hyper-realistic cover-worthy masterpiece.
```

## Macro Diorama of a Scientist's Historical Workspace

> Fotogerçekçi bilgi grafiği üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
do this for {argument name="scientist" default="Nikola Tesla"}

<instructions> Analyze: Determine the scientist responsible, the specific era, and the visual diagrams associated with the theory. 

Goal: A Macro Diorama on a Laboratory Bench. A messy, highly detailed flat-lay of a historical workspace that serves as a giant landscape for a living miniature version of the scientist.

Centerpiece: An open, antique notebook lying flat. The pages must be visible and filled with specific handwritten equations and diagrams relevant to the Input Theory. Sitting directly on top of these open pages is a tiny, hyper-realistic figurine of the scientist sitting at a proportionally tiny wooden desk, working on the very notes beneath him. 

Surroundings: The environment is cluttered with giant-scale period-accurate objects that relate to the scientist's work. This includes a newspaper from the era acting as a backdrop, a giant coffee mug leaving stains, and massive scientific tools relevant to the theory (such as compasses, lenses, or pocket watches) scattered like terrain. 

Perspective: Slightly angled 45-degree macro shot looking down at the notebook. Texture: Wood grain, scratched metal, paper texture, dust particles. Lighting: Warm, late-night library lamp lighting creating a spotlight on the tiny scientist and deep shadows behind the giant props. 

Output: one image, 4:5 aspect ratio, photorealistic historical macro photography. </instructions>
```

## Classic Literature Surveillance Machine Dioramas

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
2x2 grid, four equal panels with thin dividers. 

Each panel: a classic book turned into a miniature surveillance/administration machine diorama inside a hollowed hardcover. Visible optics, iris shutters, scanning lights, conveyors, and filing gears imply slow mechanized motion (no motion blur). Only readable text: book titles on spine/cover + small nameplates.
Panel A: 1984: brutalist ministry tower with a sweeping searchlight driven by exposed gears; a concrete “eye” iris in the façade opens/closes; streets light in scanning bands from the tower outward.
Panel B: The Trial: labyrinthine courthouse corridors as a filing-machine: drawers slide on rails; stamped paper bands loop through rollers; tiny figures queue under a rotating “verdict” drum.
Panel C: Brave New World: sterile hatchery assembly line: vial racks glide on a conveyor; a scanning lens carriage traverses on a gantry; incubation pods rise/fall on cams.
Panel D: Fahrenheit 451: fireproof archive machine: brass “flame” petals mechanically open/close around sealed shelves; book-spines retract into hidden compartments as a warning light pulses.
Style: Photoreal miniature machines embedded in classic books, cold industrial key light with subtle red/orange practical accents, gentle volumetric haze, premium studio sharpness, slate-to-charcoal seamless background, shallow DOF, centered plinth composition, 40–50mm perspective, crisp micro-detail, realistic metal/plastic/glass materials.
```

## Universal Christmas Filter Prompt Template

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Please use the subject I uploaded as the core of the image, and naturally integrate it into a realistic and believable Christmas scene.
Maintain the subject's essential identity and core recognizable features, preserve the style of the uploaded subject, without deliberate modification or templated decoration.
Actively construct the most suitable Christmas environment, light and shadow, and atmosphere based on the subject type.
If the subject is a person or animal, please match them with clothing or decorations that fit the Christmas atmosphere and scene, and design natural, context-appropriate actions, postures, and expressions, making them look like they are genuinely celebrating Christmas, not just posing.
If the subject is an item or product, the subject itself remains unchanged, and the Christmas atmosphere is created through the scene, lighting, and festive elements, which serve as embellishments without dominating the subject.
The overall image should present a warm, festive, and healing Christmas emotion, using soft holiday light and shadow with winter color contrast. The composition should be natural, realistic, and possess cinematic or advertising-level quality, making the image look like a real moment in the Christmas world.
```

{

        "action": "Perform a 'shh' gesture: one index finger held vertically and gently pressed to the center of the lips. Finger pad lightly compresses the lip surface; lips slightly parted with glossy lip oil; subtle contact shadow where finger meets lips. Expression calm, mysterious, and intimate; eyelids relaxed.",
        "location": "Dark studio backdrop, midnight mood, no visible set elements.",

            "glossy, hydrated lips with high specular highlight"
        ],
        "motion_details": "A few wind-blown hair strands sweep across the cheek and near the lips (dynamic but not messy), no heavy motion blur.",

}

"Ultra-premium editorial fashion photograph, reference-exact recreation of the provided image, identity locked to reference (NO FACE CHANGE, NO AGE CHANGE, NO BODY CHANGE).
A stylish young woman standing casually in front of an ornate traditional Rajasthani heritage doorway, perfectly centered and symmetrical composition.

Same relaxed confident stance as reference, one leg slightly crossed forward, subtle hip shift, natural posture, one arm lightly touching the wooden door frame, gaze angled slightly away from camera expression calm, effortless, authentic (NOT posed, NOT model-smile).
Face & Skin (CRITICAL):
Face must remain 100% identical to reference, bone structure, eyes, nose, lips unchanged.
Natural high-end skin texture with visible pores, micro-details, soft peach fuzz, realistic shadows no smoothing, no plastic skin, no beauty filter.
Editorial-grade realism.
Wardrobe & Fabric:
Exact same outfit as reference 
Photoreal fabric physics: natural fabric folds, accurate color absorption, realistic stitching and seams. No artificial shine.
Architecture & Background:
Traditional Rajasthani palace wall with hand-painted elephant motifs, pastel yellow and pink tones, carved wooden door with metal studs. Architectural symmetry preserved, murals crisp and undistorted, heritage textures clearly visible.
Lighting: Soft natural daylight, diffused sunlight from front-left, cinematic editorial lighting.
Balanced highlights and shadows, no harsh contrast, true-to-life skin tones, natural color temperature (~5200K).
High dynamic range, smooth tonal transitions.
Camera & Lens:
Full-frame DSLR/medium-format look
Lens: 35mm prime
Aperture: f/4 - f/5.6 (sharp from head to toe)
ISO 100
Shutter balanced for zero motion blur
Perfect vertical alignment - ZERO perspective distortion, straight lines preserved Focus & Quality:
Ultra-sharp focus, edge-to-edge clarity
No blur, no grain, no noise
True editorial sharpness, magazine-cover ready
Color grading: natural, cinematic, premium fashion editorial tone
Rendering & Output:
Photorealistic, ultra-realistic, hyper-detailed 8K clarity, professional commercial photography
quality
Clean output, no artifacts, no Al hallucinations
Looks indistinguishable from a high-budget
fashion travel shoot."
