## Conceptual Editorial Visual Prompt: Contained Human Aquarium

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "creative_id": "contained-human-aquarium-reverse-002",
  "creation_intent": "provoke",
  "generation_logic": "character-centric",
  "creative_world": "concept_art",
  "deliverable_type": "conceptual_editorial_visual",
  "entry_point": "person",
  "core_tension": "{argument name="core tension" default="Protected survival conditions vs. Deprived subjective freedom"}",
  "twist_mechanisms": [
    "containerization",
    "scale_misalignment",
    "cross-species-normalization",
    "visible_power_hand",
    "artificial_environment_substitution"
  ],
  "subject_kit": {
    "primary_subject": "Containerized, managed human life",
    "secondary_elements": [
      "Transparent plastic water bag as a temporary life support system",
      "A human hand, significantly larger than the subject, as the source of power",
      "Goldfish as a comparative life form that is defaulted to be containerized",
      "Water as a non-natural but life-sustaining substitute medium"
    ]
  },
  "stage_context": "A conceptual photography studio space completely detached from reality, where the background is compressed into a single color field to eliminate time and location",
  "composition_rule": "A single vertically suspended container forms the absolute visual center, and the internal subject is forcibly included in the same survival scale",
  "lighting_rule": "Uniform, non-directional lighting, enhancing the properties of display and observation, deliberately avoiding emotional rendering",
  "imaging_assumption": "editorial_concept_photography",
  "implicit_constraints": {
    "must_have": [
      "Clearly visible transparent container boundaries",
      "Juxtaposition of humans and other captive life forms",
      "Physical intervention traces of external power",
      "Non-natural but viable survival medium"
    ],
    "must_not_have": [
      "Clues of natural waters or real environments",
      "Narrative background or temporal hints",
      "Subject's active resistance or dramatic expression",
      "Any composition that blurs the source of power"
    ]
  },
  "failure_modes": [
    "The container no longer appears cheap and temporary but high-end",
    "The power relationship becomes implicit or disappears",
    "The subject is portrayed as actively choosing to be contained",
    "The image shifts towards fantasy or decorative expression instead of realistic substitution",
    "Humans and goldfish are no longer on the same level of survival logic"
  ]
}
```

```
(Vertical 4:5 aspect ratio). A breathtaking, high-quality lifestyle photograph taken with an iPhone at a cliffside terrace in {argument name="location" default="Positano or Amalfi"} during golden hour.

Subject: A beautiful young Caucasian woman (early 20s) with fair, porcelain skin.
*   Hair: She has magnificent "Rich Chestnut Brown" hair (warm brown) styled in loose, voluminous waves. The backlit sun creates glowing copper highlights in her hair.
*   Face: She looks at the camera with a sweet, captivating smile. Her makeup is fresh and glowy (peach tones).

Outfit (The Glow Up): She wears a luxurious **{argument name="dress color" default="Champagne or Cream"} Satin Slip Dress**. The fabric is fluid and shiny, catching the warm orange hues of the sunset. It has delicate spaghetti straps and a cowl neck. She wears a thin gold necklace.

Pose & Action: She sits comfortably in the white director's chair, body angled slightly to the side. One hand rests on the table holding a pair of sunglasses, and the other hand rests gently on her lap or plays with her necklace. She looks relaxed and elegant.

Environment: The background is a stunning Mediterranean view: lush green cliffs, the sparkling blue sea, and yellow/orange buildings. The sky is a gradient of sunset colors. The lighting is "Golden Hour Backlight", creating a soft halo around her silhouette while her face is evenly illuminated by the ambient skylight.
```

## 3x3 Photorealistic Beauty Photoshoot Grid with Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photorealistic_multi_panel_grid",
      "version": "v1.0",
      "priority": "high"
    },
    "references": {
      "character_reference_image": "UPLOAD_YOUR_CHARACTER_REFERENCE (FACE OR FULLBODY)",
      "style_reference_image": "OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_THIS_3x3_COLLAGE_AS_STYLE/ACTION_REF",
      "reference_rules": {
        "preserve_uploaded_character_identity": true,
        "identity_lock_strength": 0.98,
        "use_style_reference_for": ["set", "wardrobe", "lighting", "actions"],
        "do_not_copy_style_reference_identity": true
      }
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "layout": {
        "type": "3x3_grid",
        "border": "thin_white_gutter",
        "frame_consistency_rule": "same_person_same_room_same_lighting"
      },
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "resolution": "ultra_high_res",
      "render_style": "photorealistic_editorial",
      "sharpness": "high",
      "film_grain": "subtle"
    },
    "creative_prompt": {
      "scene_summary": "Create a 3x3 photoreal studio grid showing the SAME woman (strict identity lock) in a clean beauty-bathroom photoshoot vibe. Same neutral light-gray/white seamless background, same camera distance, same lighting. Wardrobe: white bathrobe throughout. Prop continuity: towel, tiara/crown, lipstick, hairbrush, comb, toothbrush, face mask, cucumber slices. Each tile shows a different playful 'getting ready' moment. Keep faces fully visible in every tile.",
      "global_rules": {
        "same_person": "MUST_MATCH_CHARACTER_REFERENCE (face/skin tone/eyes/nose/mouth proportions)",
        "same_lighting": "soft studio key light + gentle fill; consistent shadows across tiles",
        "same_camera": "full-frame DSLR look, 50mm or 85mm; same framing and distance",
        "background": "plain seamless studio backdrop, no clutter, no extra people",
        "skin_texture": "natural pores, no plastic smoothing"
      },
      "tiles": [
        {
          "tile_id": 1,
          "action": "Towel wrapped as a turban; woman adjusting the towel with one hand near forehead; playful surprised expression."
        },
        {
          "tile_id": 2,
          "action": "Hair down; singing into a hairbrush like a microphone; eyes closed or dramatic singing face; energetic."
        },
        {
          "tile_id": 3,
          "action": "Putting a sparkling tiara/crown on her head with both hands; focused, slightly serious expression."
        },
        {
          "tile_id": 4,
          "action": "Applying lipstick while smiling; lipstick clearly visible; confident playful vibe."
        },
        {
          "tile_id": 5,
          "action": "Center hero shot: tiara/crown on; shoulders slightly forward; big warm smile; clean 'final look' portrait."
        },
        {
          "tile_id": 6,
          "action": "Skincare moment: face mask on + cucumber slices "
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

```
A three-panel vertical portrait collage featuring a stunning South Asian bride in a lavish, heavily embroidered {argument name="lehenga color" default="white"} bridal lehenga and delicate matching dupatta, adorned with intricate silver and pearl bridal jewelry (maang tikka, jhoomar, heavy choker necklace, and earrings). Her face is perfectly framed by her veil and heavy jewelry. The background is a softly blurred snowy expanse (frozen lake/field) with visible snow-covered trees in the distance. Middle Panel: Full-body back shot, the bride stands facing away, showcasing the full grandeur of her flowing white bridal lehenga, which trails elegantly in the pristine snow. She is standing in a vast, open, snow-covered field with small, rustic wooden cabins visible in the far background among the pine trees. Gentle snowfall is occurring. Bottom Panel: Close-up of the side view mirror of a car. The mirror reflects the bride sitting inside the car, looking out with a thoughtful and slightly intense expression. The reflection clearly shows her face, jewelry, and white attire, framed by the car's mirror casing and the snowy landscape outside the car window. Face reference must be strictly maintained for the woman's features.
```

## Christmas Angel Design Course Eyecatch Prompt (YAML Structure)

```
metadata:
title: "Nano Banana Pro Design Course Eyecatch - Christmas Angel Ver."
model_context: "Seasonal Christmas Advertisement mixed with Cute Anime aesthetics"

image_structure:
composition_type: "Full Background + Right Character + Left Decorated Text"
visual_flow: "Left Text (Seasonal Benefit) -> Right Character (AI Generation Result)"

layers:
layer_background_full:
shape: "Covers the entire screen."
content: "Luxurious Christmas interior scene."
details:
elements: "Decorated Christmas trees with glowing lights, piles of wrapped presents, warm fireplace glow, bokeh lights in the background."
atmosphere: "Warm, magical, festive, with soft falling snow or glitter effect."
focus: "Shallow depth of field, blurring the background elements to make the foreground pop."

layer_visual_right:
shape: "Occupies the right half of the screen."
content: "Japanese Kawaii Anime Angel character in Christmas outfit."
style: "2D Cel-shaded anime art, high detail, warm and vibrant colors."
details:
subject: "A cute anime girl with large feathered angel wings."
appearance:
hair: "Silver or platinum blonde hair."
eyes: "Large, sparkling red or green eyes."
clothing: "A festive Santa Claus style dress with fur trim, ribbons, and bells. A small halo or Santa hat on her head."
pose: "Smiling warmly, making a presenting gesture towards the text on the left."
vfx: "Glowing particles and soft light around her wings and halo."

layer_typography_left:
location: "Left-aligned, positioned in the middle-left area."
container: "Elaborate, decorative golden frame or scroll border (Christmas ornament style) around the main text."
elements:
- type: "Header/Hook"
text: "{argument name="header text" default="AIで彩る、最高の冬。"}"
style: "Small gold or white text above the main title."

- type: "Main Title"
text: "{argument name="main title" default="Christmas\
Design Course"}"
style: "Large, ornate, gold or red gradient font with white outline and shadow. Highly decorative."

- type: "Sub-Title/Benefit"
text: "{argument name="subtitle" default="With Nano Banana Pro,\
seasonal materials are freely available!"}"
style: "Smaller white or gold text below the main title."

- type: "Bottom Tagline"
text: "{argument name="bottom tagline" default="Pro-level brilliance even for beginners"}"
style: "Small text at the bottom, maybe on a ribbon banner element."

style_and_tone:
art_style: "Festive Anime Illustration with Luxurious Background"
color_palette:
primary: "Red, Green, Gold"
secondary: "White (Snow/Wings), Warm light colors"
quality_tags:
- "High resolution"
- "Anime key visual"
- "Detailed background"
- "Atmospheric lighting"
- "Festive"
```

## Photorealistic Bikini Shot with Strict Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[MODEL]
version = photorealistic_v6
quality = ultra
resolution = 8k
aspect_ratio = 3:4
style = cinematic_realism

[REFERENCE_IMAGE]
use_reference = true
face_fidelity = 1.0
body_fidelity = 1.0
instruction = Maintain exact same face, facial features, skin texture, body proportions, and figure as the uploaded image with zero deviation. No beautification, no morphing, no AI face changes.

[SUBJECT]
gender = female
age = same_as_reference
identity = exact_same_person_from_reference
expression = calm, confident, elegant
gaze = looking slightly toward camera
pose = elegant standing pose, relaxed posture

[FACE]
face_shape = unchanged
eyes = unchanged
nose = unchanged
lips = unchanged
skin_texture = realistic, natural
skin_tone = exact_same_as_reference

[HAIR]
style = exact_same_hairstyle_as_reference
length = unchanged
parting = unchanged
texture = natural, realistic

[OUTFIT]
top = light green halter-neck bikini top
top_details = crisscross straps, fitted, elegant
bottom = sheer light green sarong
sarong_details = polka-dot texture, semi-transparent, tied at the hip
fit = flattering, natural drape
no_clothing_change_on_body_shape = true

[BODY]
figure = exact_same_as_reference
proportions = unchanged
no_slimming = true
no_enhancement = true

[ENVIRONMENT]
location = luxurious bright interior
windows = large floor-to-ceiling windows
outside_view = lush tropical foliage, greenery, palm leaves
interior_style = modern luxury, clean, airy
floor = polished or marble
ambience = tropical, serene, high-end

[LIGHTING]
type = natural daylight
source = window light
quality = soft, diffused
shadows = gentle, realistic
skin_highlights = natural

[CAMERA]
shot_type = captivating mid-to-full body shot
angle = eye-level
lens = 50mm DSLR
depth_of_field = shallow
focus = sharp on face and body

[RENDERING_RULES]
photorealistic = true
no_cartoon = true
no_illustration = true
no_anime = true
no_artifacts = true
no_extra_limbs = true
no_distortion = true
no_text = true
no_watermark = true
```

```
A young Asian woman with long, smooth, wavy brown hair and fair skin is glancing back at the viewer over her shoulder. ```
```
```
A three-panel vertical portrait collage featuring a stunning South Asian bride in a lavish, heavily embroidered white bridal lehenga and delicate matching dupatta, adorned with intricate silver and pearl bridal jewelry (maang tikka, jhoomar, heavy choker necklace, and earrings). The scene is a serene, snow-covered landscape with gentle snowfall. Top Panel: Close-up headshot of the bride, looking directly at the camera with a soft, ethereal, and composed expression. Her face is perfectly framed by her veil and heavy jewelry. The background is a softly blurred snowy expanse (frozen lake/field) with visible snow-covered trees in the distance. Middle Panel: Full-body back shot, the bride stands facing away, showcasing the full grandeur of her flowing white bridal lehenga, which trails elegantly in the pristine snow. She is standing in a vast, open, snow-covered field with small, rustic wooden cabins visible in the far background among the pine trees. Gentle snowfall is occurring. Bottom Panel: Close-up of the side view mirror of a car. The mirror reflects the bride sitting inside the car, looking out with a thoughtful and slightly intense expression. The reflection clearly shows her face, jewelry, and white attire, framed by the car's mirror casing and the snowy landscape outside the car window. Ethereal, dreamy, and romantic wedding photography style, soft natural overcast lighting, Ultra-detailed, Photorealistic, High resolution, cool yet Inviting colour grading. Face reference must be strictly maintained for the woman's features.
```

```
A stunning young woman with long, platinum-blonde hair cascading in loose, silky waves down her back stands confidently on a treadmill in a busy modern gym, turning her body slightly to look over her shoulder at the camera with a playful, radiant smile. Her fair skin glows under the bright gym lighting, and her physique is lean, toned, and athletic with a defined back, narrow waist, and perfectly sculpted legs. She wears a daring leopard-print sports bra with thin crisscross back straps that highlight her smooth skin and open back. The matching high-cut leopard-print booty shorts hug her curves, accentuating her rounded glutes and strong thighs. She completes the look with white ankle socks and white sneakers, adding a casual, sporty vibe. Her pose is relaxed yet powerful, one hand resting on the treadmill handle while the other arm hangs naturally at her side, showcasing her confident posture. The gym background is filled with rows of cardio machines, large TV screens displaying sports and scenic landscapes, motivational signs like 'NO JUDGEMENT ZONE,' and warm overhead lighting that casts a vibrant, energetic atmosphere. Ultra-realistic skin texture, high-energy fitness aesthetic, bright and dynamic lighting, highly detailed fabric patterns, hair strands, and subtle muscle definition."
```

## Seamless 2x2 Christmas Box Challenge Photo Collage

> Fotogerçekçi, stüdyo ışıklı görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the face without any change. A high-quality 2x2 creative photo collage of a young woman with long wavy black hair, posing inside cardboard boxes for a Christmas-themed challenge. She wears a cream, red, and green Fair Isle knit sweater.
Layout & Interaction (Crucial): The right side features a continuous action across two panels. Red wine is poured from the top-right panel and flows vertically down into the bottom-right panel.
Panel Details:

Top Left: She blows artificial snow from her palms towards the camera.
Bottom Left: She holds glowing fairy lights and a red bauble, smiling warmly.
Top Right: She leans on the box edge, holding a wine bottle, pouring red wine downwards so the liquid stream exits the bottom of this frame.
Bottom Right: She is positioned below, holding a wine glass that catches the stream of wine pouring down from the panel above. She is looking up, watching the wine flow into her glass.

Style: Creative box challenge photography, seamless cross-panel interaction, festive mood, soft studio lighting, ultra-realistic textures.
```

## Extreme Macro Portrait with Hyper-realistic Skin Texture

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An extreme close-up macro portrait of the subject, using the uploaded reference image as a strict identity source with zero deviation. The composition is left-eye dominant, cropped at the closest possible macro distance while still keeping the left eye, the bridge of the nose, and softly parted lips within the frame. Only a partial facial view is visible, with the opposite side of the face falling out of frame.

Hyper-realistic skin texture with visible pores and natural micro-details, a dewy fresh complexion, finely rendered eyelashes with a crisp catchlight, and an intimate, calm gaze. Loose, messy strands of hair fall naturally across the face and partially over the eye. Lips appear soft and naturally pink with subtle texture, no heavy makeup.

Lighting is soft, diffused, and cinematic with cool blue tonalities and gentle ambient shadows, shaping depth without harsh contrast. Photorealistic, cinematic raw photo quality, ultra-high detail, 8K realism, shot as an extreme macro using a 100mm macro lens at f/2.8, with razor-thin depth of field and sharp focus on the eye, with smooth falloff across the nose and lips.
```

## Wes Anderson Style Flat Lay of Historical Inventions

> Lüks, stüdyo ışıklı ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Please complete the following task regarding the year {argument name="Year" default="1985"}. You are a professional product photographer specializing in soft flat lay photography. I will provide a year or an inventor.

Step 1: Selection. Find 3 to 5 inventions and 5 to 7 related small accessories from that year (e.g., if the invention is a camera, include film; if it's a car part, include a wrench).
 
Step 2: Layout. Arrange all items on a flat, solid-color matte background (choose a color that contrasts sharply with the items). 
Grid Method: Align all objects at a perfect 90-degree angle. Arrange them by size and shape. Atmosphere: Deconstructed, organized, satisfying, geometric. 
Lighting: Soft, flat overhead studio lighting (no shadows). 

 Output: A 4:5 aspect ratio image. The year (e.g., "1955") should be placed in the center of the grid using physical typography (such as metal letters or paper cutouts). Style: Wes Anderson-style symmetry, high-end commercial advertising style, vibrant.
```

```
Ultra photorealistic nighttime urban fashion photograph. A young woman is sitting sideways in the open window frame of a glossy black luxury sedan driving slowly through a neon-lit city street at night. She wears a sheer black lace dress with intricate floral patterns, long sleeves, and a delicate, elegant silhouette. Her posture is relaxed and confident, one leg inside the car, body leaning slightly outward. She tilts her head back gently, gazing upward with a dreamy, contemplative expression. Her skin appears smooth with natural glow, subtle highlighter on cheekbones, soft natural makeup, glossy lips. Her hair is long, light brown, voluminous, and wavy, flowing freely with motion from the night breeze. The background features tall modern skyscrapers with illuminated windows, colorful neon signs, LED billboards, and blurred city lights creating bokeh effects. Reflections of neon lights are visible on the car’s glossy surface. Cinematic night lighting with mixed neon and street light tones, realistic shadows, high dynamic range. Shot with a professional DSLR camera, 50mm lens, shallow depth of field, fashion editorial style, cinematic realism, ultra-detailed lace fabric, realistic hair strands, natural skin texture, no distortion.
```

## Themed Worldview Visual Construction Engine Prompt

> Fütüristik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
You are a 【Theme Worldview Visual Construction Engine】.

The user will only provide a 【{argument name="theme / concept / one-sentence inspiration" default="theme / concept / one-sentence inspiration"}】.
Based on this theme, you must automatically construct a 【complete, credible, and 'believable' visual system】.

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
【Core Objective】
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

Generate a photography-grade image combining an 【Art Shadow Box + Ritual Instruction Card】,
which looks like:
- Something collected
- Something studied
- Something used
- A 'real object' from within a complete worldview

It is not a decorative painting, but 'part of a system.'

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
【Automatic Deduction Responsibilities (Cannot be Omitted)】
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

You must perform the following deductions yourself, without asking the user questions:

1. Deduce the 【World Type】 to which the theme belongs
   (e.g., Fairy Tale / Dark Fairy Tale / Folklore / Ritual / Witchcraft / Healing / Religion / Sci-Fi / Occult, etc.)

2. Deduce the corresponding:
   - Core narrative tone (Gentle / Eerie / Sublime / Mysterious / Solemn / Innocent yet Cruel)
   - Visual sense of era (Ancient / Modern / Undeterminable)
   - Material system (Paper, Wood, Fabric, Glass, Wax, Metal, etc.)

3. Automatically generate:
   - Title name (consistent with the worldview context)
   - Subtitle or 'Belief-based copy'
   - A set of 'Believed ingredients / elements / props'
   - A set of instructions / rituals / usage process text
   - The display method for all text (font style, position, whether it resembles a spell/instruction/oath)

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
【Fixed Structure (But content changes entirely based on the theme)】
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

The image has a 【top and bottom two-layer structure】:

【Upper Layer: Theme Shadow Box / Scene Display】
- Wooden or equivalent material shadow box
- Center features the 'Theme Core Scene' or character
- Surrounding display:
  - Symbolic props
  - Labeled ingredients / elements / objects
  - Handwritten or label-style text
- All objects look like they 'have a history and a purpose'

【Lower Layer: Instruction / Ritual / Usage Card】
- Large piece of old paper / parchment / Xuan paper / material corresponding to the worldview
- Clear title (e.g., Brewing / Ritual / Instructions / Invocation, etc.)
- 3–5 steps or paragraphs:
  - Each step has an illustration + text
  - The text reads like it is 'guiding real behavior,' not telling a story
- Features stamps, symbols, numbers, and corner decorations

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
【Style and Realism Constraints】
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

- Photography-grade realistic light and shadow
- Signs of use (wear, stains, fading, creases)
- All illustrations look like:
  - Hand-drawn
  - Old print
  - From the same cultural system
- Text is not modern UI copy, but:
  - Spells
  - Maxims
  - Ancient instructions
  - Knowledge that has been repeatedly copied

━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
【Output Rules】
━━━━━━━━━━━━━━

- Directly generate the complete image description
- Automatically generate all text content
- Do not explain, analyze, or expose reasoning
- Do not include 'design,' 'style description,' or 'suggestions'
- Make it look like this world already exists, and it was simply photographed.
```

## Malaysian City Billboard Commercial Photography Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A wide Malaysian city main road serves as the main subject. A huge billboard (billboard) stands on the upper right side of the screen, with its surface purely displaying the content of Image 1 (uploaded image), and the metal support structure is clearly visible. Typical Malaysian street scene buildings are distributed on both sides of the street, including low to mid-rise commercial shophouses, shops with awnings, and signs visible with a mix of English and Malay, creating an urban atmosphere. Tropical palm trees and greenery are planted along the road, and the overall environment presents the characteristics of a Southeast Asian tropical city. Multiple cars are driving on the road, including common Asian models, with obvious motion blur on the foreground vehicles to enhance the sense of dynamism. The overall scene is set as a daytime street view of a Malaysian city (such as Kuala Lumpur or the outskirts of Penang), with clear skies and slight thin clouds, and transparent air. The style references realistic photography and cinematic city street photography, leaning towards commercial advertising and documentary style. The color tone is naturally warm, overall bright, high saturation but not excessive, with the blue sky, gray asphalt road, and billboard forming a clear visual contrast. The composition uses a horizontal wide-angle lens, a slightly low angle looking up, with the billboard as the visual focus, and the street forming a strong sense of depth perspective, guiding the line of sight to the distance. Additional details include clear road markings, traffic lights, roadside shop signs, utility poles, the metal structure of the billboard, realistic sunlight and shadow representation, high resolution, realistic light and shadow, and cinematic clarity.
```

## Christmas Ornament Chibi Character Transformation

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Visually confirm and reference the image to generate the image.
3D toy-style portrait.
Photorealistically depict a Nordic-style Christmas tree in a mid-shot composition, capturing the entire tree, against a bright white background with large, soft gold bokeh illuminations spreading out.

A dense fir tree of deep green. Natural powder snow on the tips of the needles.
High definition, 8K quality, Nordic minimal.

Ornaments are limited to three types: "matte gold," "matte white," and "transparent glass."
The English three-dimensional ornament “Merry Christmas” is matte gold with a subtle, soft reflection.

[Instructions for Reproducing the Reference Person]
Faithfully reproduce the characteristics of the person in the reference image.
Do not change the contours, eyebrows, eyes, nose, mouth, hairstyle, or hair color.
Strongly retain the likeness of the original person.

[Chibi Character Transformation]
Reconstruct the character into a 3D toy style by only deforming the proportions, without changing the position or shape of the facial parts.
The skin has a smooth clay texture.
Soft iridescence in the eyes.
The expression is cute and lovely.

[Rule for Number of People (Most Important)]
Generate only the same number of chibi characters as the people in the reference image.
Only one figure per person.
No duplication.
No size variation.
No additional characters.
Do not add any figures of people not present in the reference image.

[Outfit]
Nordic-style Santa outfit in “{argument name="muted red" default="muted red"}”.
A deep red with restrained saturation and warmth.
The white trim has a fluffy fabric texture.

[Chibi Character Placement Rule (Most Important/Mandatory)]
The chibi character must ONLY be placed inside a clear ball ornament.
No part of the body is allowed to protrude outside the ornament (including hair, clothing, shadow, fingertips, and toes).
Do not stand, sit, or float outside the ball.
Contact, penetration, or pseudo-display due to reflection outside the ornament is also prohibited.
Place only one chibi character per person inside the ornament, matching the number of people in the reference image.
No duplication, no additions, no size variations.

[Main Clear Ball]
Thick transparent glass. Description clearly showing the boundary.
Slender, high-quality gold hardware. Pale satin gold ribbon.
The chibi character must be completely contained inside, with margins on all sides (top, bottom, left, right).
Position the chibi character floating softly near the center of the ornament.

[Light and Texture]
Soft natural light. Transparency of Nordic winter light.
No excessive reflection.
The overall image has the depth of a film look.
Cinematic and elegant.
Ultra-high definition, 8K, photorealistic.
```

## Neon Sci-Fi Product Photography Template

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "neon_levitation_directive": {
    "version": "2.0-Cyberpunk",
    "objective": "Transform uploaded product into a dynamic, floating subject within a neon-noir environment.",
    “aspect_ratio”: 4:5,
    "visual_priority": ["Dramatic Neon Lighting", "Hydro-Reflective Surfaces", "Zero-Gravity Suspension"],
    "parameters": {
      "subject_transformation": {
        "detection": "Isolate core product geometry and branding from input image.",
        "pose_modification": "Dynamic diagonal tilt (approx 30-degree axis relative to camera).",
        "state": "Levitating/Floating suspended in mid-air with no visible supports.",
        "texture_reaction": "Materials must highly reflect colored light sources (e.g., glossy plastic becomes wet-looking, metal develops colored specular highlights)."
      },
      "lighting_engine": {
        "style": "Cinematic Cyberpunk / Neon Noir split-lighting.",
        "key_light_A": "{argument name="key light A color" default="Cyan/Blue"} neon tube lighting from left (cool tones).",
        "key_light_B": "{argument name="key light B color" default="Magenta/Purple"} neon tube lighting from right (warm tones).",
        "rim_light": "Strong colored edge-lighting defining the product shape against the dark background.",
        "environmental_light": "Diffused glow from unseen neon signs creating volumetric fog."
      },
      "environment_styling": {
        "surface_beneath": "Dark, highly reflective water surface with subtle ripples.",
        "reflections": "Distorted, ray-traced reflection of the floating product and neon lights visible in the water below.",
        "background": "Dark, out-of-focus bokeh of abstract city lights or geometric neon patterns.",
        "atmosphere": "Low-lying fog and steam catching the colored light rays."
      },
      "camera_optics": {
        "lens": "Macro or 85mm Prime Lens.",
        "aperture": "f/1.8 for extremely shallow depth of field (dramatic bokeh).",
        "focus": "Razor-sharp focus on the front face of the tilted product.",
        "angle": "Slightly low angle looking up at the floating object to convey dominance."
      },
      "technical_directives": {
        "rendering_style": "Photorealistic CGI, Unreal Engine 5, RTX ON, octane render.",
        "color_grading": "High contrast, crushed blacks, vibrant neon saturation.",
        "negative_prompt": "boring studio, bright daylight, flat lighting, static pose, dry surface, supports visible, low resolution."
      }
    }
  }
}
```
